0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results for floating-point arrays\ntemplate <typename T>\nvoid print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = \"Result\") {\n    if (data.empty()) {\n        printf(\"=== RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        printf(\"Name: %s\\n\", name);\n        printf(\"Elements: 0\\n\");\n        printf(\"=== END RESULTS ===\\n\");\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const size_t N = data.size();\n    \n    // Compute statistics\n    T sum = kahan_sum(data);\n    T min_val = data[0];\n    T max_val = data[0];\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);\n        max_val = Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates\nVec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {\n    val_t u = rng.rand();\n    val_t v = rng.rand();\n    if (u + v > 1.0f) {\n        u = 1.0f - u;\n        v = 1.0f - v;\n    }\n    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;\n    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;\n    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)\n// ============================================================================\n\nval_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,\n                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {\n    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;\n    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;\n    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);\n    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);\n\n    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;\n    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;\n    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;\n    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);\n    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n\n    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle\n// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search\nbool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {\n    Vec3 dir = to - from;\n    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();\n    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;\n    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;\n\n    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {\n        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;\n\n        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);\n        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {\n            return true;  // Ray is blocked\n        }\n        return false;\n    });\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Form Factor Computation (Kij)\n// ============================================================================\n\n// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal\nval_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {\n    val_t vNorm = v.norm();\n    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;\n    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);\n}\n\n// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)\nval_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,\n                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,\n                  const Octree& octree,\n                  RandomGenerator& rng) {\n ZERO;\n\n    val_t kij = ZERO;\n\n    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {\n        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);\n        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);\n\n        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;\n\n        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;\n        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();\n        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;\n\n        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());\n        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());\n\n        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;\n\n        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);\n    }\n\n    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Tau (time delay) Computation\n// ============================================================================\n\nint computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {\n    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();\n    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Room Response Simulation State\n// ============================================================================\n\nstruct SimulationState {\n    size_t numTriangles;\n    size_t numTimesteps;\n\n    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;\n // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)\n    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source\n\n    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure\n\n    size_t sourceIndex;\n\n    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }\n    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }\n};\n\n// MT_A : 0U);\n    }\n    return x;\n}\n\ninline bool getBit(const Poly& p, size_t i) { return (p[i >> 6] >> (i & 63)) & 1U; }\ninline void setBit(Poly& p, size_t i) { p[i >> 6] |= 1ULL << (i & 63); }\n\n// Berlekamp-Massey over GF(2) on bit sequence s (length n), returns connection poly C and L\ninline void berlekampMassey(const std::vector<uint8_t>& s, Poly& C, size_t& L) {\n    size_t n = s.size();\n    size_t words = n / 64 + 2;\n    // reversed, packed sequence: rs[k] = s[n-1-k], zero padded\n    Poly rs(2 * words + 2, 0);\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) if (s[n - 1 - k]) setBit(rs, k);\n    auto window = [&](size_t off) -> uint64_t {  // 64 bits of rs starting at bit off\n        size_t q = off >> 6, r = off & 63;\n        uint64_t lo = q < rs.size() ? rs[q] : 0;\n        uint64_t hi = (q + 1) < rs.size() ? rs[q + 1] : 0;\n        return r ? (lo >> r) | (hi << (64 - r)) : lo;\n    };\n    C.assign(words, 0); Poly B(words, 0);\n    C[0] = 1; B[0] = 1; L = 0; size_t m = 1;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        // d = sum_{k=0..L} C_k s[i-k] = sum_k C_k rs[n-1-i+k]\n        size_t base = n - 1 - i;\n        uint64_t acc = 0;\n        size_t cw = L / 64 + 1;\n        for (size_t w = 0; w < cw; ++w) acc ^= C[w] & window(base + 64 * w);\n        int d = __builtin_parityll(acc);\n        if (d == 0) { ++m; continue; }\n        // C ^= B << m\n        size_t ws = m >> 6, bs = m & 63;\n        Poly T;\n        if (2 * L <= i) T = C;\n        for (size_t w = 0; w + ws < words; ++w) {\n            uint64_t v = B[w];\n            if (!v) continue;\n            C[w + ws] ^= v << bs;\n            if (bs && w + ws + 1 < words) C[w + ws + 1] ^= v >> (64 - bs);\n        }\n        if (2 * L <= i) { L = i + 1 - L; B = std::move(T); m = 1; }\n        else ++m;\n    }\n}\n\n// Characteristic polynomial phi of MT19937 (degree 19937), stored in PW words. Computed once.\ninline const Poly& charPoly() {\n    static Poly phi = [] {\n        const size_t n = 2 * MT_DEG;\n        std::vector<uint32_t> x = rawSequence(5489U, n + 2);\n        std::vector<uint8_t> s(n);\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) s[k] = x[k + 1] & 1U;\n        Poly C; size_t L;\n        berlekampMassey(s, C, L);\n        if (L != static_cast<size_t>(MT_DEG) || !getBit(C, L)) {\n            fprintf(stderr, \"MT19937 characteristic polynomial computation failed (L=%zu)\\n\", L);\n            abort();\n        }\n        Poly p(PW + 1, 0);\n        for (size_t j = 0; j <= L; ++j) if (getBit(C, L - j)) setBit(p, j);\n        return p;\n    }();\n    return phi;\n}\n\n// 64 pre-shifted copies of phi (phi << s), each PW+1 words\ninline const std::vector<Poly>& shiftedPhi() {\n    static std::vector<Poly> tab = [] {\n        const Poly& phi = charPoly();\n        std::vector<Poly> t(64, Poly(PW + 1, 0));\n        for (int s = 0; s < 64; ++s) {\n            for (int w = 0; w <= PW; ++w) {\n                uint64_t v = phi[w];\n                t[s][w] ^= v << s;\n                if (s && w + 1 <= PW) t[s][w + 1] ^= v >> (64 - s);\n            }\n        }\n        return t;\n    }();\n    return tab;\n}\n\n// Reduce p (2*PW words, degree < 2*19968) modulo phi, in place. Result has degree < 19937.\ninline void reduceMod(Poly& p) {\n    const auto& tab = shiftedPhi();\n    constexpr int topWord = MT_DEG / 64;   // 311\n    constexpr int topBit = MT_DEG % 64;    // 33\n    for (int k = 2 * PW - 1; k >= topWord; --k) {\n        for (;;) {\n            uint64_t v = p[k];\n            if (k == topWord) v &= ~((1ULL << topBit) - 1);\n            if (!v) break;\n            int i = 63 - __builtin_clzll(v);\n            size_t d = static_cast<size_t>(k) * 64 + i - MT_DEG;   // shift amount\n            size_t ws = d >> 6; int bs = static_cast<int>(d & 63);\n            const Poly& ph = tab[bs];\n            for (int w = 0; w <= PW; ++w) p[ws + w] ^= ph[w];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ninline uint64_t spread32(uint32_t v) {\n    uint64_t x = v;\n    x = (x | (x << 16)) & 0x0000FFFF0000FFFFULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 8))  & 0x00FF00FF00FF00FFULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 4))  & 0x0F0F0F0F0F0F0F0FULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 2))  & 0x3333333333333333ULL;\n    x = (x | (x << 1))  & 0x5555555555555555ULL;\n    return x;\n}\n\n// Compute x^e mod phi (result PW words, degree < 19937)\ninline Poly xPowMod(uint64_t e) {\n    Poly r(2 * PW + 1, 0);\n    r[0] = 1;\n    if (e == 0) { r.resize(PW); return r; }\n    int top = 63 - __builtin_clzll(e);\n    Poly sq(2 * PW + 1, 0);\n    for (int b = top; b >= 0; --b) {\n        // square: spread bits (degree < 19937 -> < 39874)\n        std::fill(sq.begin(), sq.end(), 0);\n        for (int w = 0; w < PW; ++w) {\n            sq[2 * w]     = spread32(static_cast<uint32_t>(r[w]));\n            sq[2 * w + 1] = spread32(static_cast<uint32_t>(r[w] >> 32));\n        }\n        if ((e >> b) & 1ULL) {  // multiply by x: shift left 1\n            uint64_t carry = 0;\n            for (int w = 0; w < 2 * PW + 1; ++w) {\n                uint64_t nc = sq[w] >> 63;\n                sq[w] = (sq[w] << 1) | carry;\n                carry = nc;\n            }\n        }\n        reduceMod(sq);\n        std::swap(r, sq);\n    }\n    r.resize(PW);\n    return r;\n}\n\n// Return a std::mt19937 seeded with `seed` and advanced by exactly `n` outputs.\ninline std::mt19937 jumpedEngine(uint32_t seed, uint64_t n) {\n    if (n < (1ULL << 21)) {\n        std::mt19937 eng(seed);\n        eng.discard(n);\n        return eng;\n    }\n    // Output k of the engine is temper(x_{624+k});
